Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

x % y % z != 3

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#offset_1: bool
  find x#offset_2: bool
new constraints:
  or([!(x#offset_2),and([!(x#offset_1),true;int(1..)]);int(1..)])
  or([and([SATInt(Offset, [x#offset_1,x#offset_2;int(1..)] [5, 7]) >= 5,SATInt(Offset, [x#offset_1,x#offset_2;int(1..)] [5, 7]) <= 7;int(1..)]);int(1..)])

--

   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])
__0
new variables:

new SAT encoding decisions:
  define(__0 <-> integer-weighted(1*x#offset_1, 2*x#offset_2) GreaterEqual 0) using None with [PbTermGroup { start: 0, end: 2, structure: BoundedBinary { lower: 0, upper: 2 } }]

   ~~> select_integer_relation ([("SAT", 18500)])

x % y % z != 3,
